\item \textbf{Milyen tanul\'asi paradigm\'at haszn\'al?} A cikk \textbf{fel\"ugyelet n\'elk\"uli (unsupervised)} \'es \textbf{f\'elig fel\"ugyelt (semi-supervised)} g\'epi tanul\'asi modelleket alkalmaz.
\item \textbf{Foglalkozik-e a cikk az kiegyens\'ulyozatlan adatk\'eszletek kezel\'es\'evel?} Az absztrakt nem eml\'iti explicit m\'odon a kiegyens\'ulyozatlan adatk\'eszletek (imbalanced datasets) kezel\'es\'ere szolg\'al\'o specifikus technik\'akat (p\'eld\'aul SMOTE), de megjegyzi, hogy a fel\"ugyelet n\'elk\"uli modelleket a h\'al\'ozatokon megjelen\H o "szapora anom\'ali\'ak" lek\"uzd\'es\'ere haszn\'alj\'ak.
\item \textbf{Foglalkozik-e a cikk a concept drift jelens\'eggel?} A rendelkez\'esre \'all\'o sz\"ovegr\'eszletekben \textbf{nem szerepel k\"ozvetlen eml\'it\'es} a concept drift jelens\'egr\H ol. Ugyanakkor az absztrakt eml\'iti, hogy vizsg\'alt\'ak a modellek \textbf{\'altal\'anos\'ithat\'os\'ag\'at (generalizability)} k\"ul\"onb\"oz\H o adatk\'eszletek k\"oz\"ott.
\item \textbf{Fontos szempont-e az \'ertelmezhet\H os\'eg (interpretability)?} Az absztrakt \'es a r\"ovid r\'eszletek \textbf{nem tesznek eml\'it\'est} az \'ertelmezhet\H os\'egr\H ol mint kiemelt szempontr\'ol.
\item \textbf{Milyen adat-el\H ofeldolgoz\'asi l\'ep\'eseket haszn\'alnak?} A cikk absztraktja \textbf{nem r\'eszletezi} az alkalmazott adat-el\H ofeldolgoz\'asi l\'ep\'eseket.
\item 
\par
\textbf{Mely adatk\'eszleteket haszn\'alt\'ak vagy tesztelt\'ek?} A tanulm\'any sor\'an n\'egy f\H o adatk\'eszletet haszn\'altak:
\par
\begin{itemize}

\item \textbf{KDD-Cup 99}
\item \textbf{NSL-KDD}
\item \textbf{CIC-IDS2017}
\item \textbf{DNP3} intrusion detection dataset (szoftveresen defini\'alt h\'al\'ozati \'es ipari IoT k\"ornyezetben).

\end{itemize}

\item \textbf{Alkalmaztak-e speci\'alis jellemz\H o-kinyer\'esi (feature engineering) m\'odszert?} Az absztrakt \textbf{nem eml\'it} speci\'alis jellemz\H o-kinyer\'esi elj\'ar\'ast.
\item 
\par
\textbf{Milyen t\'ipus\'u modellt mutat be a cikk?} A cikk \"ot k\"ul\"onb\"oz\H o fel\"ugyelet n\'elk\"uli modellt \'es ezek kombin\'aci\'oit vizsg\'alja:
\par
\begin{itemize}

\item \textbf{Generat\'iv ellens\'eges h\'al\'ozat (GAN)}
\item \textbf{Deep Belief Network (DBN)}
\item \textbf{Restricted Boltzmann Machine (RBM)}
\item \textbf{One-Class Support Vector Machine (OCSVM)}
\item \textbf{Isolation Forest (I-Forest)}.

\end{itemize}

\item \textbf{Milyen vesztes\'egf\"uggv\'enyt (loss function) haszn\'alnak?} A teljes sz\"oveg hi\'any\'aban az alkalmazott specifikus \textbf{vesztes\'egf\"uggv\'enyekr\H ol nincs inform\'aci\'o}.
\item \textbf{Milyen tanul\'asi algoritmust alkalmaznak?} A cikk a fent eml\'itett \"ot modell (GAN, DBN, RBM, OCSVM, I-Forest) algoritmusait haszn\'alja fel\"ugyelet n\'elk\"uli m\'odon az anom\'ali\'ak \'eszlel\'es\'ere. Az eredm\'enyek alapj\'an az \textbf{I-Forest \'es a DBN} ny\'ujtotta a legjobb \'altal\'anos teljes\'itm\'enyt.

\end{itemize}}
}

@article{saeedProactiveModelIntrusion2024,
  title = {A {{Proactive Model}} for {{Intrusion Detection Using Image Representation}} of {{Network Flows}}},
  author = {Saeed, Rimsha and Khaliq Qureshi, Hassaan and Ioannou, Christiana and Lestas, Marios},
  year = 2024,
  journal = {IEEE Access},
  volume = {12},
  pages = {160653--160666},
  issn = {2169-3536},
  doi = {10.1109/ACCESS.2024.3489772},
  urldate = {2026-03-04},
  abstract = {Many interconnected IoT devices driven by imperatives of efficiency and convenience often lack adequate security measures, making them susceptible to exploitation by cyber-criminals. Effective network security necessitates meticulous intrusion detection, which typically involves scrutinizing the network traffic using deep packet or stateful protocol inspection techniques. However, traditional inspection methods often require manual feature engineering, which can result in loss of payload information and thus, false alarms. In this study, a controlled testbed environment is established to capture botnet traffic. The paper introduces a detection approach that involves converting raw NetFlow data to IDX, short for `Index,' image representations. A hybrid deep learning architecture is designed, integrating VGG19 and GRU structures to learn the spatial and temporal features, respectively. The detection results show that the proposed solution achieves 98.883\% true positives rate and 0.9\% false negatives rate, surpassing conventional anomaly detection. In addition, an adaptive sliding window technique is introduced for live intrusion detection and prevention. Through iterative testing and refinement, a runtime of 0.041 ms per image and 0.00171 ms per packet is achieved, confirming the robust nature of the proposed method.},
  copyright = {https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/legalcode},
  note = {Az al\'abbi v\'alaszok a \textbf{Saeed \'es mtsai. (2024)} tanulm\'any kivonatai alapj\'an k\'esz\"ultek:
\par
\begin{itemize}

\item \textbf{Alkalmass\'ag zero-day t\'amad\'asok detekt\'al\'as\'ara:} Igen. A cikk szerint a rendszer proakt\'iv \'es k\'epes az \textbf{,,\'ujszer\H u t\'amad\'asok'' (novel attacks)} \'es a fejl\H od\H o fenyeget\'esek felismer\'es\'ere. A szerz\H ok kiemelik, hogy a hagyom\'anyos szignat\'ura-alap\'u rendszerekkel ellent\'etben az \H o megold\'asuk k\'epes kezelni azokat a fenyeget\'eseket is, amelyek nem egyeznek meg a megl\'ev\H o szignat\'ur\'akkal.
\item \textbf{Tan\'it\'asi paradigma \'es kateg\'oria:} A megold\'as a \textbf{fel\"ugyelt tanul\'as (supervised learning)} kateg\'ori\'aj\'aba tartozik, mivel a modellt konkr\'etan megnevezett oszt\'alyokra (9 t\'amad\'ast\'ipus \'es 1 norm\'al profil) tan\'itott\'ak be.
\item \textbf{Kiegyens\'ulyozatlan adathalmazok kezel\'ese:} A cikk nem t\'argyalja r\'eszletesen a kiegyens\'ulyozatlans\'ag kezel\'es\'ere szolg\'al\'o algoritmusokat (mint a SMOTE). Ugyanakkor az \'ert\'ekel\'es sor\'an \textbf{kiegyens\'ulyozott tesztk\'eszleteket} haszn\'altak (minden oszt\'alyb\'ol pontosan ugyanannyi, pl. 3750 vagy 7500 mint\'at), hogy biztos\'its\'ak az objekt\'iv m\'er\'est.
\item \textbf{Concept drift (koncepci\'ov\'alt\'as) jelens\'ege:} B\'ar a kifejez\'est n\'ev szerint nem eml\'itik, a szerz\H ok hangs\'ulyozz\'ak a modell \textbf{dinamikus alkalmazkod\'ok\'epess\'eg\'et} a v\'altoz\'o h\'al\'ozati mint\'akhoz a \textbf{GRU gating mechanizmusa} \'es az \textbf{adapt\'iv cs\'usz\'oablak-technika} r\'ev\'en.
\item \textbf{\'Ertelmezhet\H os\'eg (Interpretability):} Az \'ertelmezhet\H os\'eg \textbf{nem szerepel} a cikkben hangs\'ulyos szempontk\'ent, \'es nem eml\'itenek hozz\'a kapcsol\'od\'o technik\'akat (pl. LIME, SHAP).
\item 
\par
\textbf{Adat-el\H ok\'esz\'it\'esi l\'ep\'esek:}
\par
\begin{enumerate}

\item \textbf{Csomagszan\'al\'as (Packet Sanitization):} IP- \'es MAC-c\'imek anonimiz\'al\'asa.
\item \textbf{Hex-to-Byte konverzi\'o:} Hexadecim\'alis adatok b\'ajtsorozatt\'a alak\'it\'asa.
\item \textbf{M\'eretbe\'all\'it\'as:} Minden csomagot \textbf{384 b\'ajtra} v\'agnak vagy eg\'esz\'itenek ki (zero-padding).
\item \textbf{Flow-k\'epz\'es:} 24 egym\'ast k\"ovet\H o csomagot fognak \"ossze egyetlen forgalmi folyamba (flow).
\item \textbf{Vektoriz\'al\'as:} A b\'ajtsorozatokat t\"obbdimenzi\'os t\"ombb\'e alak\'itj\'ak.
\item \textbf{K\'epp\'e alak\'it\'as:} Az adatokat \textbf{IDX form\'atum\'u k\'epekk\'e} transzform\'alj\'ak.
\item \textbf{\'Atm\'eretez\'es:} A k\'epeket 224x224x3 m\'eretre m\'eretezik a VGG19 bemeneti k\"ovetelm\'enyei szerint.

\end{enumerate}

\item 
\par
\textbf{Felhaszn\'alt adathalmazok:}
\par
\begin{enumerate}

\item Egy saj\'at, \textbf{saj\'at testbed-en r\"ogz\'itett \'el\H o adathalmaz} (9 f\'ele botnet t\'amad\'assal).
\item \textbf{CIC-IDS2018} adathalmaz a modell \'altal\'anos\'ithat\'os\'ag\'anak valid\'al\'as\'ahoz.

\end{enumerate}

\item \textbf{Speci\'alis feature engineering:} A cikk egyik f\H o \'ujdons\'aga, hogy \textbf{elveti a manu\'alis jellemz\H o-kinyer\'est (feature engineering)}. Ehelyett a nyers NetFlow adatokat k\"ozvetlen\"ul \textbf{IDX t\'ipus\'u k\'epekk\'e} alak\'itj\'ak, meg\H orizve az \"osszes eredeti inform\'aci\'ot.
\item \textbf{Modellt\'ipus:} Egy \textbf{hibrid m\'ely tanul\'asi (deep learning)} architekt\'ura, amely egy \textbf{VGG19} (CNN) h\'al\'ozatot haszn\'al a t\'erbeli jellemz\H ok kinyer\'es\'ere \'es egy \textbf{GRU} (RNN) h\'al\'ozatot az id\H obeli f\"ugg\H os\'egek tanul\'as\'ara.
\item 
\par
\textbf{Vesztes\'egf\"uggv\'eny (Loss function):}
\par
\begin{enumerate}

\item T\"obboszt\'alyos (multi-class) oszt\'alyoz\'asn\'al: \textbf{\'Atlagos n\'egyzetes hiba (MSE)}.
\item Bin\'aris oszt\'alyoz\'asn\'al: \textbf{Bin\'aris keresztentr\'opia (BCE)}.

\end{enumerate}

\item 
\par
\textbf{Tan\'it\'asi algoritmus:}
\par
\begin{enumerate}

\item T\"obboszt\'alyos esetben: \textbf{RMSprop} optimaliz\'al\'o (0,001 tanul\'asi r\'ata, 0,5 momentum).
\item Bin\'aris esetben: \textbf{Adam} optimaliz\'al\'o (0,003 tanul\'asi r\'ata).
\item Alkalmazz\'ak tov\'abb\'a a \textbf{transzfer tanul\'ast (transfer learning)} a VGG19 eset\'eben.

\end{enumerate}

\end{itemize}

\par
A v\'alaszok a cikk biztos\'itott sz\"ovegr\'eszletei (Bevezet\'es, M\'odszertan, K\'is\'erletek, K\"ovetkeztet\'esek) alapj\'an k\'esz\"ultek, nem csup\'an metaadatokb\'ol.},

@article{sakongAdaptivePolicyBasedAnomaly2024,
  title = {An {{Adaptive Policy-Based Anomaly Object Control System}} for {{Enhanced Cybersecurity}}},
  author = {Sakong, Won and Kim, Wooju},
  year = 2024,
  journal = {IEEE Access},
  volume = {12},
  pages = {55281--55291},
  issn = {2169-3536},

  urldate = {2026-03-03},
  abstract = {Anomaly detection research focuses on identifying rare patterns derived from daily occurrences. This study introduces an innovative anomaly--object control system that utilizes adaptive policies through anomaly detection algorithms. Effectively blocking anomalous objects in real--world scenarios poses significant challenges. Therefore, we empirically validate the proposed anomaly object control methodology using the traffic history associated with malicious cyber--attacks in vulnerable network environments. We propose an anomaly object control methodology based on DeepSARSA that utilizes unsupervised anomaly detection deep learning models trained on historical data collected from an environment in which the anomaly object control system operates. Through this approach, we confirmed the adaptive policies for optimal anomaly object control. By employing the out--of--distribution detection and DeepSVDD algorithms as reward functions and comparing the results, we verified the stability of the proposed anomaly object control system. Our experimental results highlight the practical limitations of single--class anomaly detection algorithms and propose new research directions for anomaly detection.},
  copyright = {https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-nc-nd/4.0/},
  note = {A rendelkez\'esre \'all\'o forr\'asok alapj\'an az al\'abbi v\'alaszokat adom a k\'erd\'eseire a Sakong \'es Kim (2024) \'altal jegyzett cikk kapcs\'an:
\par
\begin{itemize}

\item \textbf{Alkalmas-e a megold\'as zero-day t\'amad\'asok detekt\'al\'as\'ara?} \textbf{Igen.} A cikk szerint az \textbf{anom\'alia-detekt\'al\'as} alapvet\H o c\'elja az ismeretlen vagy ritka mint\'ak azonos\'it\'asa a norm\'al adatokt\'ol val\'o elt\'er\'es alapj\'an,. A szerz\H ok kiemelik, hogy a m\'odszer k\'epes felismerni az \textbf{ismeretlen (unfamiliar) mint\'akat}, amelyek magukban foglalhatj\'ak az \'uj t\'ipus\'u anom\'ali\'akat \'es t\'amad\'asokat.
\item 
\par
\textbf{Milyen tan\'it\'asi paradigm\'at haszn\'al, \'es hogyan kategoriz\'alhat\'o?} A rendszer hibrid megk\"ozel\'it\'est alkalmaz:
\par
\begin{enumerate}

\item \textbf{Meger\H os\'it\'eses tanul\'as (Reinforcement Learning)}: A vez\'erl\H orendszer a \textbf{DeepSARSA} algoritmusra \'ep\"ul,.
\item \textbf{Fel\"ugyelet n\'elk\"uli tanul\'as (Unsupervised Learning)}: A jutalmaz\'asi f\"uggv\'enyk\'ent haszn\'alt anom\'alia-detekt\'al\'o modellek (mint a DeepSVDD) fel\"ugyelet n\'elk\"uli m\'ely tanul\'asi modellek, amelyeket norm\'al t\"ort\'eneti adatokon tan\'itottak,.

\end{enumerate}

\item \textbf{Foglalkozik-e a cikk az imbalansz adatk\'eszletek kezel\'es\'evel? Ha igen, hogyan?} \textbf{Igen.} A cikk elismeri, hogy a \textbf{kiegyens\'ulyozatlan adatk\'eszletek} (ahol a legt\"obb adat norm\'al) jelent\H os kih\'iv\'ast jelentenek. Ezt \'ugy kezelik, hogy \textbf{egyoszt\'alyos (single-class) algoritmusokat} (pl. DeepSVDD) \'es anom\'alia-detekt\'al\'ast alkalmaznak, amelyek csak a norm\'al mint\'akat tanulj\'ak meg, \'igy az anom\'ali\'akat a tanult norm\'at\'ol val\'o elt\'er\'esk\'ent azonos\'itj\'ak,.
\item \textbf{Foglalkozik-e a cikk a concept drift jelens\'eg\'evel? Ha igen, javasolnak-e r\'a megold\'ast?} A cikk eml\'iti, hogy az IoT k\"ornyezetekben a \textbf{norm\'al mint\'ak eltol\'odhatnak (shifts in pre--trained normal patterns)} a kontextu\'alis t\'enyez\H ok vagy az eszk\"oz\"ok heterogenit\'asa miatt. Megold\'ask\'ent a j\"ov\H obeni kutat\'asok ir\'anyak\'ent a \textbf{folyamatos tanul\'ast (continual learning)} vagy az \textbf{inkrement\'alis tanul\'ast} javasolj\'ak az algoritmusok friss\'it\'es\'ere.
\item \textbf{Fontos szempont-e az interpret\'alhat\'os\'ag? Ha igen, milyen technik\'akat haszn\'alnak?} A cikk metaadatai eml\'itik a \textbf{magyar\'azhat\'os\'agot (explainability)} mint kutat\'asi t\'amogat\'asi ter\"uletet, de a konkr\'et k\'is\'erleti r\'eszben a szerz\H ok \textbf{nem alkalmaznak specifikus interpret\'alhat\'os\'agi technik\'akat} (p\'eld\'aul LIME vagy SHAP).
\item 
\par
\textbf{Milyen adat-el\H ok\'esz\'it\'esi l\'ep\'eseket haszn\'alnak?} A forr\'asok az al\'abbi l\'ep\'eseket eml\'itik:
\par
\begin{enumerate}

\item \textbf{Entr\'opia-alap\'u jellemz\H o-kinyer\'es}: A forgalmi v\'altoz\'ok eloszl\'as\'anak \'es egyedi \'ert\'ekeinek sz\'am\'it\'asa.
\item \textbf{Multiclass c\'imk\'ez\'es}: A forgalmi \'allapotok oszt\'alyokba sorol\'asa a s\'avsz\'eless\'eg-ar\'anyok alapj\'an,.
\item \textbf{Temperature scaling (h\H om\'ers\'ekleti sk\'al\'az\'as)} \'es \textbf{perturbation addition (perturb\'aci\'o hozz\'aad\'asa)}: Az ODIN algoritmus eset\'eben a modell magabiztoss\'ag\'anak kalibr\'al\'as\'ara,.

\end{enumerate}

\item 
\par
\textbf{Mely adatk\'eszleteket haszn\'alt\'ak vagy tesztelt\'ek a tanulm\'anyban?} A kutat\'as sor\'an h\'arom f\H o adatk\'eszletet haszn\'altak:
\par
\begin{enumerate}

\item \textbf{CIC--IDS 2018}.
\item \textbf{CIC--DDoS 2019}.
\item Egy v\'allalati partnert\H ol sz\'armaz\'o \textbf{val\'os vil\'ag\'u adatk\'eszlet (Real-world data)}.

\end{enumerate}

\item \textbf{Alkalmaznak-e valamilyen speci\'alis feature engineering m\'odszert?} \textbf{Igen.} A bemeneti vektorok \textbf{entr\'opia-alap\'u \'es felt\'eteles entr\'opia-alap\'u jellemz\H okb\H ol} \'allnak, mint p\'eld\'aul a forr\'as/c\'el IP-c\'imek \'es portsz\'amok entr\'opi\'aja, valamint ezek egym\'ast\'ol val\'o f\"ugg\'ese,,.
\item \textbf{Milyen t\'ipus\'u modellt mutatnak be?} A bemutatott architekt\'ura egy \textbf{m\'ely meger\H os\'it\'eses tanul\'asi (Deep Reinforcement Learning)} rendszer, amely \textbf{teljesen \"osszekapcsolt (fully connected) neur\'alis h\'al\'ozatokat} haszn\'al h\'arom rejtett r\'eteggel a DeepSARSA \'es az ODIN algoritmusokhoz,.
\item 
\par
\textbf{Milyen loss functiont (vesztes\'egf\"uggv\'enyt) haszn\'alnak?}
\par
\begin{itemize}

\item A \textbf{DeepSARSA} eset\'eben \textbf{n\'egyzetes vesztes\'egf\"uggv\'enyt (squared loss)} alkalmaznak a Q-\'ert\'ek predikci\'oj\'ahoz,.
\item Az \textbf{ODIN} algoritmus tan\'it\'as\'ahoz \textbf{s\'ulyozott keresztentr\'opi\'at (weighted cross entropy)} haszn\'alnak.

\end{itemize}

\item \textbf{Milyen tan\'it\'o algoritmust alkalmaznak?} A \textbf{DeepSARSA} algoritmust haszn\'alj\'ak, amely egy \textbf{on-policy} alap\'u, id\H obeli k\"ul\"onbs\'egeken (temporal-difference) alapul\'o tanul\'asi m\'odszer,. Az \'agens az akci\'okat \textbf{\$\textbackslash epsilon\$-greedy} strat\'egia seg\'its\'eg\'evel v\'alasztja ki.

\end{itemize}

\textbf{Megjegyz\'es a hozz\'af\'er\'esr\H ol:} A v\'alaszok a Sakong \'es Kim (2024) cikk\'eb\H ol sz\'armaz\'o 61 sz\"ovegr\'eszleten alapulnak, amelyek tartalmazz\'ak a m\'odszertan, a k\'is\'erleti be\'all\'it\'asok \'es az eredm\'enyek r\'eszleteit.},

@article{saravananNovelApproachAnomaly2026,
  title = {A Novel Approach to Anomaly Detection in Critical {{CPSs}}: {{The ERXAI-MS}} Algorithm},
  shorttitle = {A Novel Approach to Anomaly Detection in Critical {{CPSs}}},
  author = {Saravanan, Prabakeran and Balaji, Annamalai and Murugan, Hemalatha and Muruganantham, Manickam and Varadharajan, Indumathi},
  year = 2026,

  journal = {Expert Systems with Applications},
  volume = {298},
  pages = {129437},

  doi = {10.1016/j.eswa.2025.129437},
  urldate = {2026-03-03},
  abstract = {At present, practical machine learning methodologies are extensively employed for the automation of detecting the intrusion available in the network. In key infrastructure scenarios involving communication strategies, the interplay between different industrial control systems and the inherent connection to the Internet environment through the Internet of Things renders them vulnerable to cyber threats. Considering the substantial volume of network traffic within critical Cyber-Physical Systems, conventional machine-learning approaches utilized for detecting anomalies prove to be ineffective. Hence, newly designed machine learning methods, with a focus on deep learning, are demonstrating effective applications in identifying and categorizing anomalies on both network and individual device levels. This article introduces an innovative Ensemble Random Explainable Artificial Intelligence Meerkat Search algorithm designed for the identification of cyber threats. To augment the effectiveness of the suggested method, it employs a dual-step process for the detection of network irregularities. During the initial phase, the approach involves data pre-processing and dimensionality reduction through the application of Kernel Principal Component Analysis to select the most suitable features. In the subsequent stage, the novel Ensemble Random Explainable Artificial Intelligence Meerkat Search algorithm is employed for classification. The effectiveness of the approach presented in this study is evaluated on diverse datasets, encompassing information collected within the Internet of Things context, specifically IoT-23 and LITNET-2020 datasets. The findings of the assessment of the suggested method are deliberated upon, including the examination of statistical significance and a comparative analysis with contemporary approaches in the field of network anomaly detection. Evaluations confirmed this robust model attained 98.56\% accuracy, 97.78\% precision, 98.2\% F1-score, and produced less FPR of 1.55\%.},
  langid = {english},
  note = {Az al\'abbi v\'alaszok a \textbf{Saravanan \'es mtsai. (2026)} tanulm\'any forr\'asanyagai alapj\'an k\'esz\"ultek:
\par
\begin{itemize}

\item \textbf{Alkalmass\'ag zero-day t\'amad\'asok detekt\'al\'as\'ara:} Igen, a rendszer alkalmas erre, mivel \textbf{anom\'aliadetekt\'al\'ason} alapul,. B\'ar a "zero-day" kifejez\'est a szerz\H ok els\H osorban a szakirodalmi \'attekint\'esben eml\'itik m\'as modellek kapcs\'an, a saj\'at ERXAI-MS modellj\"uket olyan proakt\'iv megold\'ask\'ent \'irj\'ak le, amely k\'epes az \textbf{"anom\'ali\'ak"} \'es \textbf{"rejtett h\'al\'ozati t\'amad\'asok"} azonos\'it\'as\'ara a norm\'al viselked\'est\H ol val\'o elt\'er\'es alapj\'an,,.
\item \textbf{Tan\'it\'asi paradigma \'es kateg\'oria:} A modell a \textbf{fel\"ugyelt tanul\'as (supervised learning)} kateg\'ori\'aj\'aba tartozik,,. B\'ar anom\'aliadetekt\'al\'ast v\'egez, a tan\'it\'as sor\'an c\'imk\'ezett adathalmazokat (p\'eld\'aul "Normal" \'es "Anomaly" oszt\'alyok) haszn\'al az Ensemble Random Forest tan\'it\'as\'ahoz,,.
\item \textbf{Kiegyens\'ulyozatlan adathalmazok kezel\'ese:} Igen, a cikk kiemelten foglalkozik ezzel. \item \textbf{Concept drift (koncepci\'ov\'alt\'as) jelens\'ege:} A forr\'asanyag \textbf{nem eml\'iti} n\'ev szerint a "concept drift" kifejez\'est, \'es nem javasol r\'a specifikus megold\'ast,. A hangs\'uly a statikus adathalmazokon (IoT-23, LITNET-2020) el\'ert pontoss\'agon \'es a hiperparam\'eter-optimaliz\'al\'ason van,.
\item \textbf{\'Ertelmezhet\H os\'eg (Interpretability):} Igen, ez a megold\'as egyik \textbf{kulcsfontoss\'ag\'u aspektusa} (az ERXAI n\'evben az XAI az Explainable AI-ra utal),. \item 
\par
\textbf{Adat-el\H ok\'esz\'it\'esi l\'ep\'esek:}
\par
\begin{enumerate}

\item \textbf{Normaliz\'al\'as (Normalization):} Az adatok 0 \'es 1 k\"oz\"otti tartom\'anyba val\'o sk\'al\'az\'asa a redundancia kik\"usz\"ob\"ol\'es\'ere,.
\item \textbf{Adatkiegyenl\'it\'es (Data balancing):} A hibrid SMOTE + ENN alkalmaz\'asa,.
\item \textbf{Dimenzi\'ocs\"okkent\'es (Dimensionality minimization):} A Kernel Principal Component Analysis (KPCA) haszn\'alata az optim\'alis jellemz\H ok kiv\'alaszt\'as\'ahoz,,,.

\end{enumerate}

\item \textbf{Felhaszn\'alt adathalmazok:} A tanulm\'any az \textbf{IoT-23} \'es a \textbf{LITNET-2020} benchmark adathalmazokat haszn\'alta a tesztel\'eshez,,,.
\item \textbf{Speci\'alis feature engineering:} Igen, a szerz\H ok a \textbf{Kernel Principal Component Analysis (KPCA)} m\'odszert alkalmazz\'ak a dimenzi\'ok sz\'am\'anak cs\"okkent\'es\'ere \'es a legmegfelel\H obb jellemz\H ok (features) kiv\'alaszt\'as\'ara,,,.
\item \textbf{Modellt\'ipus:} A bemutatott megold\'as egy hibrid modell, amely az \textbf{Ensemble Random Forest} (klasszikus g\'epi tanul\'asi algoritmus) algoritmust \"otv\"ozi egy metaheurisztikus optimaliz\'al\'oval (\textbf{Meerkat Search Optimization - MOA}) \'es magyar\'azhat\'o mesters\'eges intelligencia (XAI) modulokkal,,,.
\item \textbf{Vesztes\'egf\"uggv\'eny (Loss function):} A Random Forest kapcs\'an a cikk egy \textbf{predikt\'iv legkisebb n\'egyzetek alap\'u (least-square-based)} megk\"ozel\'it\'est eml\'it az emisszi\'os t\'enyez\H ok becsl\'es\'ere \'es a hiba minimaliz\'al\'as\'ara. A k\'is\'erleti eredm\'enyekn\'el "Loss" g\"orb\'eket is bemutatnak, de nem neveznek meg specifikus matematikai vesztes\'egf\"uggv\'enyt (pl. Cross-Entropy) a teljes rendszerre.
\item \textbf{Tan\'it\'asi algoritmus:} A modell az \textbf{Ensemble Random Forest} algoritmust haszn\'alja, amely t\"obb d\"ont\'esi f\'at (RT) aggreg\'al a t\'ultanul\'as cs\"okkent\'ese \'erdek\'eben,,. A hiperparam\'eterek (pl. f\'ak sz\'ama, m\'elys\'eg) finomhangol\'as\'at a \textbf{Meerkat Search Optimization (MOA)} algoritmus v\'egzi egy speci\'alis \textbf{kezdeti keres\'esi strat\'egi\'aval (initial search strategy)} kieg\'esz\'itve,,.

\end{itemize}

In this study, better and fast IDS has been proposed to detect anomaly for the cloud network environment which uses Improved squirrel search algorithm (ISSA) and Modified-deep belief network (MDBN) on the UNSW-NB15 dataset. The proposed ISSA-MDBN model for IDS reduces the training from 103.51 to 0.108 s and testing time from 29.62 to 0.054 s on the UNSW-NB15 dataset. The proposed IDS model has been compared with other existing recent approaches and the proposed approach achieves the highest accuracy of 99.8\% and lowest FAR of 0.02\%.},

@article{saygheDigitalTwinDrivenIntrusion2025,
  title = {Digital {{Twin-Driven Intrusion Detection}} for {{Industrial SCADA}}: {{A Cyber-Physical Case Study}}},

  urldate = {2026-03-03},
  abstract = {The convergence of operational technology (OT) and information technology (IT) in industrial environments, such as water treatment plants, has significantly increased the attack surface of Supervisory Control and Data Acquisition (SCADA) systems. Traditional intrusion detection systems (IDS), which focus solely on network traffic, often fail to detect stealthy, process-level attacks. This paper proposes a Digital Twin-driven Intrusion Detection (DT-ID) framework that integrates high-fidelity process simulation, real-time sensor modeling, adversarial attack injection, and hybrid anomaly detection using both physical residuals and machine learning. We evaluate the DT-ID framework using a simulated water treatment plant environment, testing against false data injection (FDI), denial-of-service (DoS), and command injection attacks. The system achieves a detection F1-score of 96.3\%, a false positive rate below 2.5\%, and an average detection latency under 500 ms, demonstrating substantial improvement over conventional rule-based and physics-only IDS in identifying stealthy anomalies. Our findings highlight the potential of cyber-physical digital twins to enhance SCADA security in critical infrastructure. In the following sections, we present the motivation and approach underlying this framework.},
  langid = {english},
  note = {Based on the excerpts provided from the article "Digital Twin-Driven Intrusion Detection for Industrial SCADA: A Cyber-Physical Case Study" by Ali Sayghe (2025), here are the answers to your questions:
\par
\begin{itemize}

\item \textbf{Is the solution suitable for detecting zero-day attacks?} \textbf{Yes.} The article explicitly states that the framework is tested against \textbf{stealthy zero-day attacks} generated using adversarial techniques inspired by GAN-based approaches,. It is designed to identify "adaptive, statistically evasive threats" that challenge traditional anomaly-based systems,.
\item \textbf{What training paradigm does it use and how can it be categorized?} The framework uses a \textbf{hybrid training paradigm}. It can be categorized as a combination of \textbf{unsupervised learning} and \textbf{adversarial training},. Specifically, the physical module uses a \textbf{one-class SVM} to model the normal process-state distribution (unsupervised), while the cyber module uses an \textbf{LSTM network} that is augmented with synthetically generated adversarial attack traces to improve generalization,,.
\item \textbf{Does the article address the handling of imbalanced datasets?} \textbf{Yes.} While not using the specific term "imbalanced," the article addresses the lack of labeled attack data by using a \textbf{one-class SVM}, which is designed to detect outliers without requiring extensive labeled attack samples. Additionally, the authors use \textbf{GAN-based data augmentation} to create synthetic attack samples to expose the models to a wider variety of anomalies.
\item \textbf{Does the article deal with the concept drift phenomenon?} \textbf{Yes.} Concept drift is monitored using the \textbf{Page--Hinkley test} applied to the residual and anomaly score distributions,. If drift is detected, the system prioritizes \textbf{periodic retraining} of the models (which typically occurs every 24 hours) and notifies the operators.
\item \textbf{Is interpretability considered an important aspect?} \textbf{Yes}, but not through traditional XAI techniques like LIME or SHAP. Instead, the authors emphasize \textbf{operator situational awareness} through a \textbf{3D visualization layer} created in \textbf{Unity 3D}. This provides a real-time graphical mimic of the plant to help operators intuitively understand anomalies, bridging the gap between technical alerts and engineering decisions,.
\item 
\par
\textbf{What data preprocessing steps are used?} The preprocessing steps include:
\par
\begin{enumerate}

\item \textbf{Sliding window processing}: Network traffic is processed in 60-timestep (one-minute) windows.
\item \textbf{Feature extraction}: Computing packet sizes, function codes, and inter-arrival times for network data.
\item \textbf{Statistical summarization}: Calculating mean, variance, and maximum values over the windows,.
\item \textbf{Residual calculation}: Computing the difference between actual sensor readings and Digital Twin predictions: \$r(t) = \textbar y\_\textbraceleft sensor\textbraceright (t) - y\_\textbraceleft DT\textbraceright (t)\textbar\$.
\item \textbf{Smoothing}: Anomaly scores are smoothed using a \textbf{moving average filter} (window size = 3) to reduce false triggers during transients.

\end{enumerate}

\item \textbf{Which datasets were used or tested in the study?} The study used a \textbf{custom 72-hour SCADA dataset} generated from a \textbf{simulated water treatment plant (WTP) environment},. The simulation was built using Python (SimPy) and Scapy. The authors also reference established datasets like \textbf{SWaT, WADI, and BATADAL} as benchmarks for comparison in the literature,.
\item \textbf{Is there any special feature engineering method applied?} \textbf{Yes.} The framework uses \textbf{Physics-Guided Hybrid Detection}, which involves engineering \textbf{process residuals} (the difference between physical sensors and the digital twin's physics-based model) as primary features for the anomaly detector,.
\item 
\par
\textbf{What type of model is presented?} The article presents a \textbf{hybrid anomaly detection engine} consisting of:
\par
\begin{enumerate}

\item A \textbf{three-layer LSTM network} with an \textbf{attention mechanism} for the cyber module,.
\item A \textbf{one-class SVM} with a \textbf{radial basis function (RBF) kernel} for the physical module,.

\end{enumerate}

\item \textbf{What loss function is used?} The provided text does not explicitly name the specific loss function used for the LSTM network. For the physical module, it utilizes the standard optimization formulation for a \textbf{one-class SVM} with parameters \$\textbackslash nu = 0.01\$ and \$\textbackslash gamma = 0.1\$.
\item \textbf{What training algorithm is applied?} The models undergo \textbf{periodic retraining every 24 hours} using a mix of new operational data and synthetic samples. The detection threshold and fusion weights are optimized using \textbf{grid search} and \textbf{ROC curve analysis},. It also incorporates \textbf{adversarial training} via GAN-generated traces.

@article{shafieianMultilayerStackingEnsemble2023,
  title = {Multi-Layer Stacking Ensemble Learners for Low Footprint Network Intrusion Detection},
  author = {Shafieian, Saeed and Zulkernine, Mohammad},
  year = 2023,
  month = aug,
  journal = {Complex \& Intelligent Systems},
  volume = {9},
  number = {4},
  pages = {3787--3799},
  issn = {2198-6053},
  doi = {10.1007/s40747-022-00809-3},
  urldate = {2025-06-13},
  abstract = {Machine learning has become the standard solution to problems in many areas, such as image recognition, natural language processing, and spam detection. In the area of network intrusion detection, machine learning techniques have also been successfully used to detect anomalies in network traffic. However, there is less tolerance in the network intrusion detection domain in terms of errors, especially false positives. In this paper, we define strict acceptance criteria, and show that only very few ensemble learning classifiers are able to meet them in detecting low footprint network intrusions. We compare bagging, boosting, and stacking techniques, and show how methods such as multi-layer stacking can outperform other ensemble techniques and non-ensemble models in detecting such intrusions. We show how different variations on a stacking ensemble model can play a significant role on the classification performance. Malicious examples in our dataset are from the network intrusions that exfiltrate data from a target machine. The benign examples are captured by network taps in geographically different locations on a big corporate network. Among hundreds of ensemble models based on seven different base learners, only three multi-layer stacking models meet the strict acceptance criteria, and achieve an F1 score of 0.99, and a false-positive rate of 0.001. Furthermore, we show that our ensemble models outperform different deep neural network models in classifying low footprint network intrusions.},
  langid = {english},
  keywords = {Anomaly detection,Computer Crime,Computer Science,Data Mining,Ensemble learning,Low footprint intrusion,Machine Learning,Mobile and Network Security,Network intrusion detection,Stacking ensemble learning,Statistical Learning},

\par
\textbf{Adat-el\H ok\'esz\'it\'esi l\'ep\'esek:}
\par
\begin{enumerate}

\item \textbf{Adatt\'ipus-konverzi\'o:} Kategorikus adatok numerikus \'abr\'azol\'ass\'a alak\'it\'asa.

@article{shanaAnomalybasedIntrusionDetection2025,
  title = {Anomaly-Based Intrusion Detection System Based on {{SMOTE-IPF}}, {{Whale Optimization Algorithm}}, and Ensemble Learning},

  abstract = {Nowadays, cybersecurity is a major worldwide problem. Intrusion detection systems (IDS) help guarantee network security by detecting malicious entries from legitimate entries in network traffic data. IDS has considerable potential for detecting dynamic cyber threats, identifying abnormalities, and identifying malicious conduct within the network. In this paper, we propose Machine Learning (ML) models with an emphasis on the Synthetic Minority Over-sampling Technique (SMOTE) with Iterative Partitioning Filter (IPF) for class imbalance and the Whale Optimization Algorithm (WOA) for feature selection. Class imbalance often results in poorly constructed ML models prioritizing the majority class. In addition, the absence of feature selection can lead to higher computational complexity without impacting performance accuracy. This study uses Bagging, AdaBoost, Extreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) and Extra Trees Classifier as classification models. The two widely used datasets to assess the proposed method are NLS-KDD and UNSW-NB15. The K-Fold cross-validation technique trains this model to minimize potential overfitting. These models are evaluated based on performance metrics such as accuracy, precision, recall, and F1-score. The experimental results demonstrate that the Extra Trees Classifier significantly outperforms the baseline models and achieves accuracy values of 99.9\% for the NSL-KDD dataset and 97\% for the UNSW-NB 15 dataset and outperforms all evaluation measures compared to baseline models for multi-classification of the IDS.},

@article{soltaniMultiagentAdaptiveDeep2024,
  title = {A Multi-Agent Adaptive Deep Learning Framework for Online Intrusion Detection},
  author = {Soltani, Mahdi and Khajavi, Khashayar and Jafari Siavoshani, Mahdi and Jahangir, Amir Hossein},
  year = 2024,
  month = may,
  journal = {Cybersecurity},
  volume = {7},
  number = {1},
  pages = {9},
  issn = {2523-3246},
  doi = {10.1186/s42400-023-00199-0},
  urldate = {2026-03-03},
  abstract = {The network security analyzers use intrusion detection systems (IDSes) to distinguish malicious traffic from benign ones. The deep learning-based (DL-based) IDSes are proposed to auto-extract high-level features and eliminate the time-consuming and costly signature extraction process. However, this new generation of IDSes still needs to overcome a number of challenges to be employed in practical environments. One of the main issues of an applicable IDS is facing traffic concept drift, which manifests itself as new (i.e.~, zero-day) attacks, in addition to the changing behavior of benign users/applications. Furthermore, a practical DL-based IDS needs to be conformed to a distributed (i.e.~, multi-sensor) architecture in order to yield more accurate detections, create a collective attack knowledge based on the observations of different sensors, and also handle big data challenges for supporting high throughput networks. This paper proposes a novel multi-agent network intrusion detection framework to address the above shortcomings, considering a more practical scenario (i.e., online adaptable IDSes). This framework employs continual deep anomaly detectors for adapting each agent to the changing attack/benign patterns in its local traffic. In addition, a federated learning approach is proposed for sharing and exchanging local knowledge between different agents. Furthermore, the proposed framework implements sequential packet labeling for each flow, which provides an attack probability score for the flow by gradually observing each flow packet and updating its estimation. We evaluate the proposed framework by employing different deep models (including CNN-based and LSTM-based) over the CIC-IDS2017 and CSE-CIC-IDS2018 datasets. Through extensive evaluations and experiments, we show that the proposed distributed framework is well adapted to the traffic concept drift. More precisely, our results indicate that the CNN-based models are well suited for continually adapting to the traffic concept drift (i.e.~, achieving an average detection rate of above 95\% while needing just 128 new flows for the updating phase), and the LSTM-based models are a good candidate for sequential packet labeling in practical online IDSes (i.e.~, detecting intrusions by just observing their first 15 packets).},
  langid = {english},
  note = {A Soltani \'es munkat\'arsai (2024) \'altal jegyzett cikk alapj\'an a feltett k\'erd\'esekre az al\'abbi v\'alaszok adhat\'oak:
\par
\begin{itemize}

\item \textbf{Alkalmass\'ag zero-day t\'amad\'asok detekt\'al\'as\'ara:} Igen, a bemutatott megold\'ast kifejezetten az \textbf{\'uj (azaz zero-day) t\'amad\'asok} \'es a forgalmi mint\'ak v\'altoz\'as\'anak (koncepci\'ov\'alt\'as) kezel\'es\'ere tervezt\'ek,. A keretrendszer folyamatos tanul\'asi (continual learning) algoritmusokat haszn\'al, hogy az IDS-t proakt\'ivan adapt\'alja az ismeretlen fenyeget\'esekhez,.
\item \textbf{Tan\'it\'asi paradigma \'es kateg\'oria:} A megold\'as a \textbf{fel\"ugyelt tanul\'as (supervised learning)} kateg\'ori\'aj\'aba tartozik. B\'ar anom\'aliadetekt\'al\'ast v\'egez, a folyamat sor\'an \textbf{c\'imk\'ezett adathalmazokat} (mint a CIC-IDS2017/2018) \'es c\'imk\'ezett mint\'akat haszn\'alnak a modellek friss\'it\'es\'ehez \'es a tud\'asmegoszt\'ashoz,,.
\item \textbf{Kiegyens\'ulyozatlan adathalmazok kezel\'ese:} Igen, a cikk foglalkozik ezzel a k\'erd\'essel. \item \textbf{Concept drift (koncepci\'ov\'alt\'as) jelens\'ege:} Igen, a cikk egyik k\"ozponti t\'em\'aja a h\'al\'ozati forgalom koncepci\'ov\'alt\'asa,. A szerz\H ok egy \textbf{t\"obbl\'epcs\H os m\'ely folyamatos tanul\'asi (continual learning) algoritmust} javasolnak a helyi adapt\'aci\'ohoz, valamint egy \textbf{feder\'alt tanul\'asi (federated learning)} megk\"ozel\'it\'est a k\"ul\"onb\"oz\H o \'agensek k\"oz\"otti tud\'asmegoszt\'ashoz,,.
\item \textbf{\'Ertelmezhet\H os\'eg (Interpretability):} A forr\'asanyag \textbf{nem t\'argyalja az \'ertelmezhet\H os\'eget} mint a saj\'at modelljeik egyik funkci\'oj\'at. Az XAI (magyar\'azhat\'o MI) technik\'akat csak a kapcsol\'od\'o munk\'ak (pl. Abou El Houda et al., 2022) ismertet\'es\'en\'el eml\'itik, a saj\'at modellj\"ukn\'el nem alkalmaznak SMOTE vagy LIME elj\'ar\'asokat,.
\item 
\par
\textbf{Adat-el\H ok\'esz\'it\'esi l\'ep\'esek:}
\par
\begin{enumerate}

\item Tiszta forgalmi tartalom kinyer\'ese \textbf{PCAP form\'atumb\'ol},.
\item A csomagm\'eret \textbf{200 b\'ajtra} val\'o be\'all\'it\'asa.
\item A folyamat (flow) m\'eret\'enek \textbf{100 csomagra} val\'o korl\'atoz\'asa.
\item Egy \textbf{20 000 dimenzi\'os bemeneti vektor} (flow matrix) l\'etrehoz\'asa.
\item Adatok feloszt\'asa tan\'it\'o (64\%), valid\'aci\'os (16\%) \'es tesztel\H o (20\%) halmazokra.

\end{enumerate}

\item \textbf{Felhaszn\'alt adathalmazok:} A k\'is\'erletek sor\'an a \textbf{CIC-IDS2017} \'es a \textbf{CSE-CIC-IDS2018} benchmark adathalmazokat haszn\'alt\'ak,,.
\item \textbf{Speci\'alis feature engineering:} Nem alkalmaznak manu\'alis jellemz\H o-kinyer\'est. A m\'ely tanul\'as alap\'u megk\"ozel\'it\'es l\'enyege \'eppen az, hogy a h\'al\'ozat \textbf{automatikusan vonja ki a magas szint\H u jellemz\H oket} a nyers adatokb\'ol,,,.
\item \textbf{Modellt\'ipus:} A cikk \textbf{m\'ely tanul\'asi (deep learning)} modelleket mutat be, konkr\'etan \textbf{CNN-alap\'u} (konvol\'uci\'os neur\'alis h\'al\'ozat) \'es \textbf{LSTM-alap\'u} (hossz\'u-r\"ovid t\'av\'u mem\'oria) architekt\'ur\'akat haszn\'alnak \'es hasonl\'itanak \"ossze,,,.
\item \textbf{Vesztes\'egf\"uggv\'eny:} Els\H odlegesen a \textbf{bin\'aris keresztentr\'opi\'at (binary cross-entropy)} haszn\'alj\'ak. Emellett a tud\'asmegoszt\'asi f\'azisban egy \textbf{tud\'asdesztill\'aci\'os vesztes\'egf\"uggv\'enyt (knowledge distillation loss)} \'es regulariz\'aci\'os tagokat (Fisher inform\'aci\'os m\'atrix) is alkalmaznak,,.
\item \textbf{Tan\'it\'asi algoritmus:} A keretrendszer egy \textbf{folyamatos tanul\'asi algoritmust} (Algorithm 1) \'es egy \textbf{feder\'alt tanul\'asi elj\'ar\'ast} (Algorithm 3) alkalmaz,. A neur\'alis h\'al\'ozatok s\'ulyainak optimaliz\'al\'as\'ahoz az \textbf{Adam optimaliz\'al\'ot} haszn\'alj\'ak.

\end{itemize}

\par

@article{tianCollaborativeDataPlaneControlPlane2025,
  title = {A {{Collaborative Data-Plane}} and {{Control-Plane Architecture}} for {{Real-Time Anomaly Detection}}},
  author = {Tian, Xiao and Liu, Jinjiang and Qi, Qinglei and Li, He and Ma, Xiaopu and Zhao, Cong},
  year = 2025,
  journal = {IEEE Access},
  volume = {13},

  issn = {2169-3536},
  doi = {10.1109/ACCESS.2025.3631738},
  urldate = {2026-03-03},
  abstract = {Programmable data planes enable real-time traffic monitoring and line-rate response. However, their limited computational capabilities restrict deep packet inspection. To address this limitation, we propose a hybrid framework that offloads feature-rich packet sequence representations from the data plane to the control plane for intelligent decision-making. The system extracts multi-packet flow features directly within programmable switches and transmits structured representations to a controller equipped with a Transformer-LSTM prediction model. The controller estimates a confidence score for each flow and translates it into dynamic forwarding rules, which are then deployed back to the switches to determine whether the corresponding packets should be forwarded. We implement this framework on the BMv2 switch and evaluate its performance using a recent distributed denial-of-service (DDoS) attack dataset. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves a flow feature collection rate of 97.56\%, a detection accuracy of 99.99\%, and an online inference latency of just 896 ns. These findings indicate the potential of the framework for real-time traffic classification and rapid response under high-throughput network conditions.},
  copyright = {https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/legalcode},
  note = {A megadott forr\'asdokumentum ("A Collaborative Data-Plane and Control-Plane Architecture for Real-Time Anomaly Detection") alapj\'an az al\'abbi v\'alaszokat adom a k\'erd\'eseire:
\par
\begin{itemize}

\item \textbf{Zero-day t\'amad\'asok \'eszlel\'ese:} Az eredeti sz\"ovegben a "zero-day" kifejez\'es nem szerepel. Azonban a szerz\H ok a megold\'ast \textbf{anom\'alia-alap\'u \'eszlel\'esi keretrendszerk\'ent} defini\'alj\'ak, amely k\'epes a \textbf{"fejl\H od\H o h\'al\'ozati fenyeget\'esekre"} (evolving network threats) val\'o gyors \'es pontos v\'alaszad\'asra. Az anom\'alia-detekt\'al\'as elm\'eletileg alkalmas az ismeretlen (zero-day) t\'amad\'asok felismer\'es\'ere a norm\'al forgalomt\'ol val\'o elt\'er\'es alapj\'an.
\item \textbf{Tan\'it\'asi paradigma:} A modell \textbf{fel\"ugyelt tanul\'ast (supervised learning)} alkalmaz. Ez onnan tudhat\'o, hogy a modellt c\'imk\'ezett adatokon (benignus \'es t\'amad\'o forgalom) tan\'itott\'ak, \'es a teljes\'itm\'eny\'et olyan mutat\'okkal \'ert\'ekelt\'ek, mint a konf\'uzi\'os m\'atrix, a prec\'izi\'o \'es a recall.
\item \textbf{Kiegyens\'ulyozatlan adathalmazok kezel\'ese:} A cikk \textbf{nem eml\'it konkr\'et adatkiegyenl\'it\H o technik\'at} (p\'eld\'aul SMOTE-ot). Ehelyett olyan \'ert\'ekel\'esi mutat\'okat alkalmaz, mint az \textbf{F1-score} \'es a \textbf{Matthews-f\'ele korrel\'aci\'os egy\"utthat\'o (MCC)}, amelyek megb\'izhat\'obb k\'epet adnak a teljes\'itm\'enyr\H ol kiegyens\'ulyozatlan adatok eset\'en is.
\item \textbf{Concept drift (koncepci\'ov\'alt\'as):} A forr\'as \textbf{nem t\'argyalja} a concept drift jelens\'eg\'et, \'es nem javasol r\'a megold\'ast.
\item \textbf{Interpret\'alhat\'os\'ag:} Az interpret\'alhat\'os\'ag \textbf{nem jelenik meg fontos szempontk\'ent} a tanulm\'anyban. A szerz\H ok nem eml\'itenek olyan technik\'akat, mint a LIME vagy a SHAP; a hangs\'uly a pontoss\'agon, a sk\'al\'azhat\'os\'agon \'es az alacsony k\'esleltet\'esen van.
\item 
\par
\textbf{Adat-el\H ok\'esz\'it\'esi l\'ep\'esek:}
\par
\begin{enumerate}

\item \textbf{Csomag\'ertelmez\'es (Parsing):} Strukt\'ur\'alt mez\H ok \'es protokollfejl\'ecek kinyer\'ese a nyers byte-folyamb\'ol.
\item \textbf{Jellemz\H o-kinyer\'es:} P4-alap\'u \'allapotg\'ep (FSM) haszn\'alata a kapcsol\'on bel\"ul a csomagsz\'aml\'al\'ok, id\H ob\'elyegek \'es viselked\'esi jelz\H ok nyomon k\"ovet\'es\'ere.
\item \textbf{Cs\'usz\'oablakos m\'odszer (Sliding window):} A tan\'it\'asi adatok gener\'al\'asa nyers PCAP f\'ajlokb\'ol cs\'usz\'oablak alkalmaz\'as\'aval a flow-szint\H u id\H obeli szemantika meg\H orz\'ese \'erdek\'eben.
\item \textbf{M\'atrix-k\'epz\'es:} Minden csomagot 14 t\'erbeli jellemz\H ovel \'es egy id\H ob\'elyeggel reprezent\'alnak, \'igy egy \$N \textbackslash times 15\$-\"os bemeneti m\'atrixot hoznak l\'etre.
\item \textbf{Id\H ob\'elyegek kiz\'ar\'asa:} Az online tesztel\'es sor\'an az id\H ob\'elyeg-alap\'u jellemz\H oket ideiglenesen kihagyj\'ak az emul\'alt k\"ornyezet pontatlans\'agai miatt.

\end{enumerate}

\item \textbf{Dual-hash mechanizmus:} CRC16-ot haszn\'alnak a regiszter-indexel\'eshez \'es CRC32-t "\H ork\'ent" (signature/guard) a hash-\"utk\"oz\'esek \'eszlel\'es\'ere.

\item \textbf{Gradiens v\'ag\'as} (gradient clipping) a stabilit\'as \'erdek\'eben.

\end{itemize}

@article{toureFrameworkDetectingZeroday2024,
  title = {A Framework for Detecting Zero-Day Exploits in Network Flows},
  author = {Tour{\'e}, Almamy and Imine, Youcef and Semnont, Alexis and Delot, Thierry and Gallais, Antoine},
  year = 2024,
  month = jun,
  journal = {Computer Networks},
  volume = {248},
  pages = {110476},
  issn = {13891286},
  doi = {10.1016/j.comnet.2024.110476},
  urldate = {2026-03-03},
  abstract = {Zero-day attack detection solutions aim to proactively identify unknown threats targeting valuable assets within a given system. While many Intrusion Detection System (IDS) solutions leverage learning techniques to build novel attack detection systems, they often focus on enhancing accuracy for specific attack types, overlooking the potential for multiple attack scenarios. Therefore, we introduce a novel framework for detecting zero-day attacks that evade current detection systems. Our framework enhances attack identification and qualification through a hybrid learning approach, where supervised learning ensures detection of known attacks and unsupervised learning. It encompasses intrusion detection phases from data collection to new attack class detection by identifying anomalies in real-time network flow data. Unsupervised learning, which involves grouping similar data points into clusters, establishes minimum distances within these clusters. This process triggers cluster division when certain thresholds are reached. Finally, an online supervised learning process validates our approach's effectiveness in identifying anomalies associated with zero-day attack flows. This approach significantly reduces the False Detection Rate (FDR) without solely focusing on optimizing machine learning (ML) and deep learning (DL) algorithms hyper-parameters. We evaluated our framework on two datasets: one from a real industrial context at IBM and the NSL-KDD dataset. The results demonstrate our framework's ability to detect anomalies in previously zero-day attack targets. On average, we identified 71 anomalous flows per target, achieving an overall average online learning accuracy of 98.4\% for the IBM dataset and 96.6\% for the NSL-KDD dataset, thereby validating the detection of these new attack scenarios.},
  langid = {english},
  note = {A \textbf{Tour\'e \'es munkat\'arsai (2024)} \'altal jegyzett, \emph{"A framework for detecting zero-day exploits in network flows"} c\'im\H u cikk alapj\'an az al\'abbi v\'alaszokat adom a k\'erd\'eseire:
\par
\begin{itemize}

\item \textbf{Alkalmass\'ag zero-day t\'amad\'asok detekt\'al\'as\'ara:} Igen, a cikk egy olyan keretrendszert mutat be, amelyet kifejezetten a \textbf{zero-day t\'amad\'asok proakt\'iv azonos\'it\'as\'ara} terveztek. A megold\'as c\'elja az olyan fenyeget\'esek \'eszlel\'ese, amelyek elker\"ulik a jelenlegi v\'edelmi rendszereket.
\item 
\par
\textbf{Tan\'it\'asi paradigma \'es kateg\'oria:} A rendszer egy \textbf{hibrid tanul\'asi megk\"ozel\'it\'est (hybrid learning approach)} alkalmaz. Ez a k\"ovetkez\H oket \"otv\"ozi:
\par
\begin{itemize}

\item \textbf{Fel\"ugyelt tanul\'as (supervised learning):} Az ismert t\'amad\'asok oszt\'alyoz\'as\'ara (CNN, DT, RF, KNN, NB).
\item \textbf{Fel\"ugyelet n\'elk\"uli tanul\'as (unsupervised learning):} Klaszterez\'es \'es anom\'aliadetekt\'al\'as c\'elj\'ab\'ol (K-Means).
\item \textbf{Online/Folyamatos tanul\'as (online learning):} Az \'uj t\'amad\'asi mint\'ak (zero-day) val\'os idej\H u adapt\'aci\'oj\'ahoz.

\end{itemize}

\item \textbf{Kiegyens\'ulyozatlan adathalmazok kezel\'ese:} Igen, a szerz\H ok foglalkoznak ezzel. Megjegyzik, hogy a hagyom\'anyos modellek (pl. CNN, LSTM) pontoss\'aga cs\"okkenhet \textbf{kiegyens\'ulyozatlan adatok} eset\'en. Ennek kezel\'es\'ere egy \textbf{boosting (er\H os\'it\H o) technik\'at} alkalmaznak, amely t\"obb algoritmust (DT, RF, KNN, NB) kombin\'al, hogy minimaliz\'alj\'ak a t\'eves detekt\'al\'asokat \'es jav\'its\'ak a pontoss\'agot.
\item \textbf{Concept drift (koncepci\'ov\'alt\'as) jelens\'ege:} B\'ar a cikk nem haszn\'alja explicit m\'odon a "concept drift" kifejez\'est, foglalkozik a forgalmi mint\'ak \'es a botnetek gyors v\'altoz\'as\'aval. Megold\'ask\'ent az \textbf{online/inkrement\'alis tanul\'ast} javasolj\'ak, amely lehet\H ov\'e teszi a modell friss\'it\'es\'et az \'uj adatok alapj\'an an\'elk\"ul, hogy a teljes adathalmazon \'ujra kellene tan\'itani a rendszert.
\item \textbf{\'Ertelmezhet\H os\'eg (Interpretability):} Az \'ertelmezhet\H os\'eg mint technikai szempont (pl. LIME, SHAP) \textbf{nem szerepel kiemelten} a cikkben, \'es nem alkalmaznak SMOTE elj\'ar\'ast sem. Ugyanakkor az \textbf{unsupervised klaszterez\'es (K-Means)} vizu\'alis reprezent\'aci\'ot ny\'ujt az adatokr\'ol, \'es a l\'etrehozott \textbf{korrel\'aci\'os t\'abl\'azat} seg\'it \"osszekapcsolni a klasztereket az ismert t\'amad\'asi oszt\'alyokkal, ami bizonyos szint\H u magyar\'azhat\'os\'agot biztos\'it.
\item 
\par
\textbf{Adat-el\H ok\'esz\'it\'esi l\'ep\'esek:}
\par
\begin{enumerate}

\item \textbf{Adatgy\H ujt\'es} raw csomagokb\'ol (SPAN, TAP) vagy k\"uls\H o forr\'asokb\'ol (NetFlow, sFlow, jFlow).
\item \textbf{Jellemz\H o-kinyer\'es \'es dimenzi\'ocs\"okkent\'es:} Az eredeti kb. 150 jellemz\H ot 14-re cs\"okkentik.
\item \textbf{Adattiszt\'it\'as:} A null \'ert\'ekeket tartalmaz\'o rekordok elt\'avol\'it\'asa.
\item \textbf{Konverzi\'o:} Az IP-c\'imek b\'ajtonk\'enti hexadecim\'alis \'atalak\'it\'asa, \"osszef\H uz\'ese, majd decim\'alis \'ert\'ekre v\'alt\'asa.
\item \textbf{K\'odol\'as:} A nem numerikus jellemz\H ok (pl. protokoll, flow direction) \textbf{OneHotEncoder} seg\'its\'eg\'evel t\"ort\'en\H o bin\'ariss\'a alak\'it\'asa.
\item \textbf{Normaliz\'al\'as:} \textbf{Standardiz\'al\'as (Standardization)} alkalmaz\'asa a legjobb sz\'etv\'alaszthat\'os\'ag \'erdek\'eben.

\end{enumerate}

\item 
\par
\textbf{Felhaszn\'alt adathalmazok:}
\par
\begin{enumerate}

\item Egy val\'os ipari k\"ornyezetb\H ol sz\'armaz\'o \textbf{IBM adathalmaz} (QRadar Network Insight seg\'its\'eg\'evel gy\H ujtve).
\item Az \'allami szf\'er\'aban elterjedt \textbf{NSL-KDD} benchmark adathalmaz.

\end{enumerate}

\item \textbf{Speci\'alis feature engineering:} Igen. A szerz\H ok a h\'al\'ozati folyamat (flow) jellemz\H oit h\'arom f\H o csal\'adba sorolj\'ak: \textbf{Asset} (eszk\"oz), \textbf{Flow} (folyamat) \'es \textbf{Exchanged data and rate} (cser\'elt adat \'es sebess\'eg) jellemz\H ok. C\'eljuk egy ,,univerz\'alis minimalista'' jellemz\H ok\'eszlet l\'etrehoz\'asa a hat\'ekony val\'os idej\H u feldolgoz\'ashoz.
\item 
\par
\textbf{Modellt\'ipus:} A keretrendszer egy \textbf{hibrid modell}, amely az al\'abbiakat \"otv\"ozi:
\par
\begin{itemize}

\item \textbf{M\'ely tanul\'as:} 1D-CNN (Egydimenzi\'os konvol\'uci\'os neur\'alis h\'al\'ozat).
\item \textbf{Klasszikus g\'epi tanul\'as:} D\"ont\'esi f\'ak (DT), Random Forest (RF), K-Nearest Neighbors (KNN) \'es Naive Bayes (NB) egy\"uttes alkalmaz\'asa.
\item \textbf{Klaszterez\'es:} K-Means algoritmus.

\end{itemize}

\item \textbf{Vesztes\'egf\"uggv\'eny (Loss function):} A cikk nem nevez meg klasszikus matematikai vesztes\'egf\"uggv\'enyt (pl. Cross-Entropy) a CNN tan\'it\'as\'ahoz. Ehelyett az \textbf{F1-score-t} haszn\'alj\'ak a boosting algoritmusok s\'ulyoz\'as\'ahoz, a zero-day \'eszlel\'esn\'el pedig a \textbf{Silhouette-pontsz\'amot} alkalmazz\'ak a klaszterek min\H os\'eg\'enek \'es a sz\'etv\'al\'asi k\"usz\"ob\'ert\'eknek (\$d-min\$) a meghat\'aroz\'as\'ahoz.
\item 
\par
\textbf{Tan\'it\'asi algoritmus:}
\par
\begin{enumerate}

\item \textbf{Supervised f\'azis:} 1D-CNN tan\'it\'asa \'es boosting technika az algoritmusok f\'uzi\'oj\'aval.
\item \textbf{Unsupervised f\'azis:} K-Means algoritmus alkalmaz\'asa v\'eletlenszer\H u centroid-inicializ\'al\'assal \'es az \textbf{Elbow-m\'odszerrel} meghat\'arozott klasztersz\'ammal.
\item \textbf{Anom\'aliadetekt\'al\'as:} Outlier-anal\'izis a Silhouette-pontsz\'amb\'ol sz\'am\'itott \textbf{minim\'alis t\'avols\'ag (\$d-min\$)} alapj\'an.
\item \textbf{Online tanul\'as:} A modell val\'os idej\H u friss\'it\'ese az \'uj anom\'ali\'ak alapj\'an, amennyiben az nem l\'epi \'at a meghat\'arozott degrad\'aci\'os k\"usz\"ob\"ot (\$deg-th\$).

\end{enumerate}

\end{itemize}

\textbf{Megjegyz\'es:} A v\'alaszok a cikk biztos\'itott sz\"ovegr\'eszletei alapj\'an k\'esz\"ultek, a teljes dokumentum tartalma hozz\'af\'erhet\H o volt a kidolgoz\'ashoz.},

@article{truongMetaCIDSPrivacyPreservingCollaborative2023,
  title = {{{MetaCIDS}}: {{Privacy-Preserving Collaborative Intrusion Detection}} for {{Metaverse}} Based on {{Blockchain}} and {{Online Federated Learning}}},
  shorttitle = {{{MetaCIDS}}},
  author = {Truong, Vu Tuan and Le, Long Bao},
  year = 2023,
  journal = {IEEE Open Journal of the Computer Society},
  volume = {4},
  pages = {253--266},
  issn = {2644-1268},
  doi = {10.1109/OJCS.2023.3312299},
  urldate = {2026-03-03},
  abstract = {Metaverse is expected to rely on massive Internet of Things (IoT) connections so it inherits various security threats from the IoT network and also faces other sophisticated attacks related to virtual reality technology. As traditional security approaches show various limitations in the large-scale distributed metaverse, this paper proposes MetaCIDS, a novel collaborative intrusion detection (CID) framework that leverages metaverse devices to collaboratively protect the metaverse. In MetaCIDS, a federated learning (FL) scheme based on unsupervised autoencoder and an attention-based supervised classifier enables metaverse users to train a CID model using their local network data, while the blockchain network allows metaverse users to train a machine learning (ML) model to detect intrusion network flows over their monitored local network traffic, then submit verifiable intrusion alerts to the blockchain to earn metaverse tokens. Security analysis shows that MetaCIDS can efficiently detect zero-day attacks, while the training process is resistant to SPoF, data tampering, and up to 33\% poisoning nodes. Performance evaluation illustrates the efficiency of MetaCIDS with 96\% to 99\% detection accuracy on four different network intrusion datasets, supporting both multi-class detection using labeled data and anomaly detection trained on unlabeled data.},
  copyright = {https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-nc-nd/4.0/},
  note = {A rendelkez\'esre bocs\'atott forr\'asok alapj\'an az al\'abbi v\'alaszokat adhatjuk a MetaCIDS keretrendszerrel kapcsolatban:
\par
\begin{itemize}

\item \textbf{Alkalmass\'ag zero-day t\'amad\'asok \'eszlel\'es\'ere:} Igen, a rendszer alkalmas az ilyen t\'ipus\'u t\'amad\'asok felder\'it\'es\'ere. A megold\'as egy \textbf{nem fel\"ugyelt autoencodert (DAE)} haszn\'al anom\'alia-\'eszlel\'esre, amely k\'epes azonos\'itani a tan\'it\'o adathalmazban nem szerepl\H o, ismeretlen t\'amad\'asi mint\'akat (azaz a zero-day t\'amad\'asokat) a magas rekonstrukci\'os hiba alapj\'an,,.
\item \textbf{Tan\'it\'asi paradigma:} A keretrendszer \textbf{f\'elig fel\"ugyelt (semi-supervised)} tanul\'ast alkalmaz,. Ez k\'et r\'eszb\H ol \'all: egy c\'imk\'ezetlen adatokon tan\'itott \textbf{nem fel\"ugyelt autoencoderb\H ol} \'es egy c\'imk\'ezett adatokon alapul\'o \textbf{fel\"ugyelt oszt\'alyoz\'ob\'ol},. A tan\'it\'asi folyamat eg\'esze a \textbf{feder\'alt tanul\'as (Federated Learning)} modellj\'ere \'ep\"ul,.
\item \textbf{Kiegyens\'ulyozatlan adathalmazok kezel\'ese:} Igen, a cikk foglalkozik ezzel a k\'erd\'essel. A szerz\H ok az adathalmazok kiegyens\'ulyozatlans\'aga miatt a teljes\'itm\'eny ki\'ert\'ekel\'es\'en\'el az egyszer\H u pontoss\'ag (accuracy) mellett a \textbf{precizit\'ast (precision) \'es a felid\'ez\'est (recall)} is alkalmazz\'ak. Emellett megeml\'itik, hogy bizonyos adathalmazokn\'al (pl. NSL-KDD) m\'ar a kiad\'ok elv\'egezt\'ek a mintav\'etelez\'est az egyens\'uly jav\'it\'asa \'erdek\'eben.
\item \textbf{Concept drift (koncepci\'ov\'alt\'as) jelens\'ege:} B\'ar a "concept drift" kifejez\'est nem haszn\'alj\'ak sz\'o szerint, a cikk az \textbf{online feder\'alt tanul\'as} (online FL) r\'ev\'en kezeli ezt a probl\'em\'at. A modell nem egy statikus adathalmazra t\'amaszkodik, hanem \textbf{folyamatosan friss\"ul} a leg\'ujabb h\'al\'ozati forgalomb\'ol sz\'armaz\'o adatok alapj\'an, \'igy alkalmazkodva a v\'altoz\'o forgalmi mint\'akhoz,,.
\item \textbf{\'Ertelmezhet\H os\'eg (interpretability):} Az \'ertelmezhet\H os\'eg mint els\H odleges szempont nem jelenik meg hangs\'ulyosan a saj\'at modell tervez\'es\'en\'el, de a rendszer tartalmaz egy \textbf{figyelem-alap\'u s\'ulyoz\'o modult (attention-based weighting module)}. Ez a modul minden jellemz\H oh\"oz s\'ulyt rendel, \'igy azonos\'ithat\'o, hogy mely jellemz\H ok b\'irnak a legnagyobb hat\'assal a d\"ont\'eshozatalra. SMOTE vagy LIME technik\'ak alkalmaz\'as\'at a forr\'as nem eml\'iti.
\item 
\par
\textbf{Adat-el\H ok\'esz\'it\'esi l\'ep\'esek:}
\par
\begin{enumerate}

\item \textbf{Dimenzi\'ocs\"okkent\'es:} Az encoder modul a h\'al\'ozati forgalmat egy alacsony dimenzi\'os reprezent\'aci\'oba (k\'odba) k\'epezi le,.
\item \textbf{Adatv\'edelem (DP zaj):} A helyi tan\'it\'as sor\'an a gradiensekhez \textbf{differenci\'alis adatv\'edelmi (Gaussian DP) zajt} adnak az adatsziv\'arg\'as megel\H oz\'ese \'erdek\'eben.
\item \textbf{K\'odol\'as:} A h\'al\'ozati folyamatok t\"om\"or\'it\'ese az autoencoder encoder egys\'eg\'evel,.

\end{enumerate}

\item \textbf{Felhaszn\'alt adathalmazok:} A tanulm\'any sor\'an n\'egy h\'al\'ozati bet\"or\'esjelz\'esi adathalmazt haszn\'altak a tesztel\'eshez: \textbf{CSE-CIC-IDS2018, CIC-IDS2017, NSL-KDD \'es UNSW-NB15}.
\item \textbf{Speci\'alis jellemz\H o-kinyer\'esi (feature engineering) m\'odszerek:} A szerz\H ok kijelentik, hogy elosztott rendszerekben neh\'ez manu\'alis jellemz\H o-kinyer\'est alkalmazni, ez\'ert \textbf{nem haszn\'alnak speci\'alis manu\'alis m\'odszereket}. Ehelyett az \textbf{autoencoder encoder\'et} haszn\'alj\'ak automatikus jellemz\H o-kinyer\'esre, \'es egy \textbf{figyelem-alap\'u s\'ulyoz\'o modult} \'ep\'itettek be, amely automatikusan megtanulja a legfontosabb jellemz\H ok s\'ulyoz\'as\'at,.
\item \textbf{A modell t\'ipusa:} A MetaCIDS egy \textbf{m\'elytanul\'asi (deep learning)} alap\'u modell, amely egy \textbf{m\'ely autoencodert (DAE)} \'es egy \textbf{figyelem-alap\'u (attention-based) neur\'alis h\'al\'ozatot} kombin\'al egy blockchain-alap\'u feder\'alt keretrendszerben,,.
\item \textbf{Vesztes\'egf\"uggv\'eny:} Az autoencoder eset\'eben a modell a rekonstrukci\'os hiba minimaliz\'al\'as\'ara t\"orekszik, amelyhez a \textbf{n\'egyzetes k\"oz\'ephib\'at (Mean Squared Error - MSE)} haszn\'alja,.
\item \textbf{Alkalmazott tan\'it\'asi algoritmus:} A keretrendszer \textbf{sztochasztikus gradiens ereszked\'est (Stochastic Gradient Descent - SGD)} haszn\'al a helyi tan\'it\'ashoz. A glob\'alis modell aggreg\'aci\'oj\'ahoz a decentraliz\'alt blockchain h\'al\'ozaton a m\'ergez\'es-t\H ur\H o \textbf{Multi-Krum} algoritmust, valamint a \textbf{pBFT konszenzus mechanizmust} alkalmazz\'ak,,.

\end{itemize}

@article{wangApplicationDualchannelHighorder2025,
  title = {Application of Dual-Channel High-Order Graph Neural Network for Intrusion Detection with Semisupervised Learning},
  author = {Wang, Zhendong and Bao, Jiaqi and Zeng, Zhiyuan and Chan, Sammy},
  year = 2025,
  month = sep,
  journal = {Cluster Computing},

  doi = {10.1007/s10586-025-05304-1},
  urldate = {2026-03-03},
  abstract = {Graph neural networks (GNNs) are deep learning methods for graphs that differ from traditional deep learning methods in that they can be applied to non-Euclidean data, such as user categorization in social networks and molecular structures. In computer networks, network traffic can be expressed very naturally in terms of graph structures, such as the GNN field of intrusion detection. There are many GNN-based intrusion detection methods, but some issues have not been fully explored. (1) More attention has been given to the topological information of the graph, while the characteristics of the network traffic itself have not been fully explored. (2) Most intrusion detection methods based on GNNs consider only the characteristic information of their first-order neighbors while ignoring the characteristic information of high-order neighbors in the graph. Most methods rely on high-quality labeled samples, which entail significant costs. To address these issues, we propose a semi-supervised network intrusion detection system (NIDS) method based on dual-channel high-order graph neural networks (DHGNNs). Our model employs a dual-channel architecture, with one channel dedicated to node attribute learning. This channel employs a deep learning approach to fully exploit the attribute feature information inherent to network traffic. In the other channel, we design a high-order message-passing mechanism to efficiently pass and aggregate the feature information of the high-order neighboring nodes and use an attention mechanism to obtain the node embeddings. Finally, we obtain the final node embedding by using channel attention to fuse the features of the two channels. The experimental results on four datasets show that our method achieves 98.63--99.86\% accuracy in anomaly detection with a small amount of labeled data, as well as state-of-the-art multiclassification performance on these four datasets, especially on NF-BoT-IoT-V2, which is much higher than that of state-of-the-art algorithms.},
  langid = {english},

In recent years, many researchers have applied deep learning technology to intrusion detection systems and obtained fairly strong recognition results. However, most experiments have used old datasets, so they could not reflect the latest attack information. In this paper, a current state of the CSE-CIC-IDS2018 dataset and standard evaluation metrics has been employed to evaluate the proposed mechanism. After preprocessing the dataset, six models---deep neural network (DNN), convolutional neural network (CNN), recurrent neural network (RNN), long short-term memory (LSTM), CNN + RNN and CNN + LSTM---were constructed to judge whether network traffic comprised a malicious attack. In addition, multi-classification experiments were conducted to sort traffic into benign traffic and six categories of malicious attacks: BruteForce, Denial-of-service (DoS), Web Attacks, Infiltration, Botnet, and Distributed denial-of-service (DDoS). Each model showed a high accuracy in various experiments, and their multi-class classification accuracy were above 98\%. Compared with the intrusion detection system (IDS) of other papers, the proposed model effectively improves the detection performance. Moreover, the inference time for the combinations of CNN + RNN and CNN + LSTM is longer than that of the individual DNN, RNN and CNN. Therefore, the DNN, RNN and CNN are better than CNN + RNN and CNN + LSTM for considering the implementation of the algorithm in the IDS device.},
  langid = {english},
  note = {Based on the provided excerpts from the article \textbf{"Network Anomaly Intrusion Detection Based on Deep Learning Approach"} (Wang et al., 2023), here are the answers to your questions:
\par
\begin{itemize}

\item \textbf{Is the solution suitable for detecting zero-day attacks?} The article describes the proposed system as an \textbf{intrusion detection system (IDS)} used to monitor network traffic for \textbf{abnormal behavior}. While it does not explicitly use the term "zero-day," it states the system is designed to "conduct effective defense against \textbf{new ransomware}" and to "more effectively detect \textbf{new and complex types of network attacks}".
\item \textbf{What training paradigm does it use, and how can it be categorized?} The model uses a \textbf{supervised learning} paradigm. It relies on a dataset with labeled samples (e.g., "benign" or specific attack types like "Botnet") to train and evaluate the models.
\item \textbf{Does the article address the handling of imbalanced datasets?} The article acknowledges the problem of an \textbf{unbalanced number of attack types} in its "Related Work" section, mentioning that other researchers have used the \textbf{Synthetic Minority Oversampling Technique (SMOTE)}. However, for its own methodology, the article focuses on \textbf{data cleaning and preprocessing} to handle "meaningless data" and "outliers" rather than using an algorithmic balancing technique like SMOTE. The resulting test sets remain significantly imbalanced (e.g., "Web Attack" makes up only 0.01\% of the data).
\item \textbf{Does the article deal with the concept drift phenomenon?} The provided excerpts \textbf{do not mention or propose a solution for concept drift} in the context of the authors' own model.
\item \textbf{Is interpretability considered an important aspect?} Interpretability is \textbf{not highlighted as a central goal} of this study. While the article mentions SMOTE in the "Related Work" section, it is cited as a method used by other researchers (Karatas et al.) to improve detection efficiency, not as an interpretability technique used by the authors.
\item 
\par
\textbf{What data preprocessing steps are used?} The article lists a comprehensive set of preprocessing steps:
\par
\begin{enumerate}

\item \textbf{Data Merging:} Combining ten CSV files into a single file.
\item \textbf{Data Cleaning:} Removing "non-attack" labels, "meaningless" features (e.g., Timestamp, Flow ID, IP addresses), features where all values were zero, and duplicate data.
\item \textbf{Outlier Padding:} Using \textbf{mode filling} for "NaN" (Not a Number) and "Inf" (Infinity) values.
\item \textbf{Data Transformation:} Converting text labels into numerical format (binary and multi-class) and applying \textbf{one-hot encoding}.
\item \textbf{Data Split:} Dividing the data into training-validation (80\%) and testing (20\%) sets using the \textbf{holdout method}.
\item \textbf{Numerical Normalization:} Applying \textbf{standardization} (mean of 0 and SD of 1) and \textbf{natural logarithmic transformation}.

\end{enumerate}

\item \textbf{Which datasets were used or tested in the study?} The study used the \textbf{CSE-CIC-IDS2018 dataset}. The authors note that older datasets like KDD Cup 1999 and NSL-KDD are "outdated and unreliable" for modern attack detection.
\item \textbf{Is there any special feature engineering method applied?} The authors state that \textbf{deep learning can learn and process features by itself}, reducing the need for manual feature engineering. However, they did perform manual \textbf{feature removal}, discarding 14 features that were either irrelevant to classification (like timestamps and IP addresses) or contained only zero values.
\item \textbf{What type of model is presented?} The article presents and compares \textbf{six deep learning models}: \textbf{Deep Neural Network (DNN)}, \textbf{Convolutional Neural Network (CNN)}, \textbf{Recurrent Neural Network (RNN)}, \textbf{Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM)}, and hybrid combinations of \textbf{CNN + RNN} and \textbf{CNN + LSTM}.
\item \textbf{What loss function is used?} The excerpts \textbf{do not explicitly name a specific loss function} (such as Cross-Entropy), though they specify that the output layers use \textbf{Sigmoid} for binary classification and \textbf{SoftMax} for multi-class classification.
\item \textbf{What training algorithm is applied?} The models were trained using the \textbf{back propagation method}. The training was implemented using \textbf{TensorFlow 2.2.0} and accelerated with \textbf{NVIDIA GPUs}.

\end{itemize}

\par

@article{wangSpatialtemporalKnowledgeDistillation2024,
  title = {Spatial-Temporal Knowledge Distillation for Lightweight Network Traffic Anomaly Detection},
  author = {Wang, Xintong and Wang, Zixuan and Wang, Enliang and Sun, Zhixin},
  year = 2024,
  month = feb,
  journal = {Computers \& Security},
  volume = {137},
  pages = {103636},
  issn = {01674048},
  doi = {10.1016/j.cose.2023.103636},
  urldate = {2026-03-03},
  abstract = {Deep learning-based network traffic anomaly detection methods have been the mainstream approaches to enhancing the accuracy performance of Network Intrusion Detection Systems (NIDSs). However, there are several problems that remain to be addressed in practical scenarios. First, the memory and computing power of intelligent terminals restrict the deployment of computationally intensive deep learning methods. Second, the depth and width of representations are of central significance for the accuracy of detection, at the cost of memory consumption and computational complexity. Third, the long tail effect spawned by the category imbalance of network traffic is prevalent in real-world fine-grained anomaly detection tasks. Therefore, we propose a Spatial-Temporal Knowledge Distillation (STKD) algorithm framework for lightweight network traffic anomaly detection to tackle the challenges. Integrating multi-scale One-Dimensional Convolutional Neural Network (1D CNN) and Long Short-Term Memory Network (LSTM), and adopting identity mapping, we propose a Multi-Scale Spatial-Temporal Residual Network (MSSTRNet) as the teacher model for deep spatial-temporal feature extraction of network traffic. Based on Knowledge Distillation (KD), we compress MSSTRNet to the lightweight student model named LENet which is suitable for deployment. Introducing Focal Loss (FL) instead of Cross Entropy (CE) Loss into the KD process, we attempt to alleviate the long tail effect in the fine-grained anomaly detection tasks. Experiments demonstrate the superiority of our proposed methods on accuracy performance, memory consumption and computation complexity.},

@article{wangUnifiedFrameworkHybrid2025,
  title = {A {{Unified Framework}} for {{Hybrid Network Intrusion Detection}}},
  author = {Wang, Yan and Cao, Sheng and Li, Jingwei and Zhang, Xiaosong},
  year = 2025,
  month = dec,
  journal = {IEEE Transactions on Network and Service Management},
  volume = {22},
  number = {6},
  pages = {5328--5347},
  issn = {1932-4537, 2373-7379},
  doi = {10.1109/TNSM.2025.3609854},
  urldate = {2026-03-03},
  abstract = {Lately, hybrid network intrusion detection systems (HNIDSs) have progressed significantly. Through the cascade or ensemble of multiple machine learning models, HNIDS benefits from each model and achieves better performance. A widely adopted framework for designing HNIDS consists of two models: a misuse detector and an anomaly detector. However, (1) benign traffic must be analyzed by both models, reducing inference speed; (2) the misuse detector performs dual functionalities, leading to suboptimal accuracy; (3) deploying the misuse and anomaly detectors on two devices introduces substantial latency and restricts distributed deployment. In this article, we propose a unified framework called AUF. To solve (1), we deploy the anomaly detector in the first stage rather than the second, which improves inference speed. To solve (2), we employ two independent models to implement the misuse detector's functionality, enhancing overall accuracy. To solve (3), we ensure that the different models operate independently, supporting distributed deployment. To demonstrate the effectiveness of the AUF framework, we implement XGBoost for detection and classification and propose an adaptive k-nearest neighborhood-based approach to achieve accurate discrimination. We also introduce zero-shot learning to showcase the framework's customized model. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of the AUF framework and methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/wangyann2000/A-Unified-Framework-for-Hybrid-Network-Intrusion-Detection.},
  copyright = {https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/Xplorehelp/downloads/license-information/IEEE.html},
  annotation = {Access to Full Paper is not available},
  note = {A megadott forr\'asok k\"oz\"ul az els\H ok\'ent szerepl\H o, \textbf{,,A Unified Framework for Hybrid Network Intrusion Detection''} (Wang \'es t\'arsai, 2025. janu\'ar) c\'im\H u cikk alapj\'an az al\'abbi v\'alaszokat adom a k\'erd\'eseire:
\par
\begin{itemize}

\item \textbf{Alkalmas-e a megold\'as zero-day t\'amad\'asok detekt\'al\'as\'ara?} \textbf{Igen}, a cikk szerint a keretrendszer alkalmas lehet erre, mivel a szerz\H ok bevezetik a \textbf{zero-shot learning} (nullapontos tanul\'as) alkalmaz\'as\'at, amely lehet\H ov\'e teszi a modell sz\'am\'ara az olyan kateg\'ori\'ak felismer\'es\'et, amelyeket a tan\'it\'as sor\'an nem l\'atott.
\item \textbf{Milyen tan\'it\'asi paradigm\'at haszn\'al, \'es hogyan kategoriz\'alhat\'o?} A keretrendszer egy \textbf{hibrid} megk\"ozel\'it\'est alkalmaz. Az absztrakt eml\'iti az \textbf{XGBoost} haszn\'alat\'at detekt\'al\'asra \'es oszt\'alyoz\'asra, ami egy \textbf{fel\"ugyelt tanul\'asi (supervised learning)} algoritmus, ugyanakkor a \textbf{zero-shot learning} \'es egy \textbf{adapt\'iv k-legk\"ozelebbi szomsz\'ed (k-NN)} alap\'u m\'odszer is a r\'esze.
\item \textbf{Foglalkozik-e a cikk az imbalansz adatk\'eszletek kezel\'es\'evel?} A rendelkez\'esre \'all\'o absztrakt \'es metaadatok \textbf{nem tesznek eml\'it\'est} a kiegyens\'ulyozatlan adatk\'eszletek kezel\'es\'er\H ol.
\item \textbf{Foglalkozik-e a cikk a concept drift jelens\'eg\'evel?} A forr\'ask\'ent megadott sz\"ovegr\'eszletek \textbf{nem eml\'itik} a concept drift jelens\'eg\'et.
\item \textbf{Fontos szempont-e az interpret\'alhat\'os\'ag? (Megjegyz\'es: a forr\'asok k\"oz\"ott szerepl\H o 6-os sz\'am\'u dokumentum egy survey az interpret\'alhat\'o anom\'alia-detekt\'al\'asr\'ol, de az nem azonos ezzel az els\H odleges cikkel).
\item \textbf{Milyen adat-el\H ok\'esz\'it\'esi l\'ep\'eseket haszn\'alnak?} Az absztrakt \textbf{nem r\'eszletezi} a konkr\'et adat-el\H ok\'esz\'it\'esi l\'ep\'eseket.
\item \textbf{Mely adatk\'eszleteket haszn\'alt\'ak vagy tesztelt\'ek a tanulm\'anyban?} A dokumentum ,,sz\'elesk\"or\H u k\'is\'erleteket'' (extensive experiments) eml\'it, de az absztrakt szintj\'en \textbf{nem nevezi meg a konkr\'et adatk\'eszleteket}.
\item \textbf{Alkalmaznak-e valamilyen speci\'alis feature engineering m\'odszert?} Igen, a szerz\H ok egy \textbf{adapt\'iv k-legk\"ozelebbi szomsz\'ed (k-nearest neighborhood) alap\'u megk\"ozel\'it\'est} javasolnak a pontos megk\"ul\"onb\"oztet\'es el\'er\'ese \'erdek\'eben.
\item \textbf{Milyen t\'ipus\'u modellt mutatnak be?} Egy \textbf{hibrid h\'al\'ozati behatol\'asjelz\H o rendszer (HNIDS)} keretrendszert, az \textbf{AUF}-ot. Ez mag\'aban foglal egy anom\'alia-detektort \'es egy vissza\'el\'es-detektort (misuse detector), technikai szinten pedig az \textbf{XGBoost}-ot, az \textbf{adapt\'iv k-NN}-t \'es a \textbf{zero-shot learning}-et kombin\'alja.
\item \textbf{Milyen loss functiont (vesztes\'egf\"uggv\'enyt) haszn\'alnak?} A rendelkez\'esre \'all\'o absztrakt \textbf{nem eml\'iti} a haszn\'alt vesztes\'egf\"uggv\'enyt.
\item \textbf{Milyen tan\'it\'o algoritmust alkalmaznak?} Az absztrakt az \textbf{XGBoost} algoritmust \'es az \textbf{adapt\'iv k-NN} alap\'u elj\'ar\'ast eml\'iti meg.

\end{itemize}

\textbf{Fontos megjegyz\'es:} A fenti v\'alaszok kiz\'ar\'olag a cikk \textbf{absztraktj\'ab\'ol \'es metaadataib\'ol sz\'armaznak}, mivel a \textbf{teljes sz\"oveg nem \'allt rendelkez\'esre} a forr\'asok k\"oz\"ott. With the rapid development of the network in the direction of diversification, the traditional intrusion detection system (IDS) has problems such as low accuracy and high false alarm rate, which are difficult to guarantee the current network security. In this paper, the author proposes a method that combines conditional variational autoencoder (CVAE) and long-short-term memory (LSTM) network to identify and detect abnormal flow, and then some key technologies of traffic detection model is discussed. At present, the main problems in network traffic anomaly detection include imbalanced data distribution and low detection efficiency of traditional models. Due to the fact that most network detection data often has the characteristics of a small number of attack category samples and imbalanced data distribution, CVAE is used to enhance and expand the attack samples to obtain balanced data samples in this paper, and then the LSTM network is used for anomaly identification and detection. In order to prove the superiority of the model, the author evaluates the model through the accuracy, precision, recall and F1.
